The camera zooms into the bearded pirate as he starts to reel in the fishing line.</p>\n<p>The scene fades to black, and a golden “Sea of Thieves: Anniversary Update” logo appears onscreen.</p>\n<p>The scene opens on the side of a Galleon ship in green livery, with a crew of pirates manning three cannons on its right-hand side. They fire three cannonballs in rhythmic succession with great explosions and bursts of smoke.</p>\n<p>We cut to see the cannonballs fired in the previous shot strike a second Galleon, red sails raised, anchored just offshore from a small tropical island. The cannonballs strike the red ship’s mast.</p>\n<p>From a bird’s eye view we see the mast begin to topple, then cutting to a view from the deck, we see the mast fall and just as it would strike the viewer, the \"Sea of Thieves: The Arena\" logo explodes onto the screen with a flash of light.</p>\n<p>The scene cuts to five ships in a tropical blue sea. Two of the ships appear to be permanently moored, with red beacons of smoke emanating from the top of the single remaining mast. Makeshift pontoons and jetties have been set up at water level next to these ships.</p>\n<p>The other three ships liveries of different colours and are engaging in sea battle. The banners have the same colours and iconography as the ships we previously saw in battle.</p>\n<p>A bright flash turns the screen to black with a golden \"Sea of Thieves: Tall Tales, Shores of Gold\" logo.</p>\n<p>The scene cuts to the interior of an underground stone structure. The red and blue mottled fish splashes back and forth at the limit of the line’s length.</p>\n<p>We cut to a lone sloop bobbing in rough seas, the sun setting in the distance.
